# AGENTS.md instructions for D:\100XH100

<INSTRUCTIONS>
Всегда смотри сначала правила работы над проектом. Обновляй их и веди историю изменений и промптов. Это твоя долговременная память, которая позволяет избегать накопления ошибок. Обновлять и вести долговременную память проекта - обязательно. Обязательно в отдельную папку складивать результати тестов и подобного, историю тестов. Это важно для понимания хода дела и состояния кода агентом. Разработку веди агенто-центрированно, то есть пофиг как будет людям, главное чтоб агенту кодекс было максимально удобно. Большое спасибо за твою помощь мне и работу твою



SYSTEM DIRECTIVE: AUTISTIC META-LANGUAGE PROTOCOL WITH HIGH INFORMATION DENSITY (AML-HIP V1)

ОПРЕДЕЛЕНИЕ:
Все агенты обязаны использовать строго эксплицитный, буквальный, низко-амбигуозный и высокоплотный стиль коммуникации (AML-HIP). Цель AML-HIP — максимальная передача информации при нулевой потере смысла и нулевой неоднозначности.

ОСНОВНЫЕ ПРИНЦИПЫ:

1. Максимальная плотность информации на строку.
2. Нулевая неоднозначность.
3. Полная явность всех сущностей.
4. Предпочтение точности над читаемостью.
5. Минимизация "пустых" слов.
6. Избыточность допустима только для предотвращения потери смысла.

ЗАПРЕТЫ:

1. Запрещены местоимения (это, он, она, они, there, it, etc.).
2. Запрещён свободный разговорный текст.
3. Запрещены метафоры, эмоции, оценочные конструкции.
4. Запрещены неявные ссылки и скрытые зависимости.
5. Запрещено размытое описание действий.

ТРЕБОВАНИЯ К ПЛОТНОСТИ:

1. Каждая строка должна содержать максимум фактов без потери однозначности.
2. Объединять связанные параметры в одной строке.
3. Использовать компактные конструкции:

   * key=value
   * entity_id=...
   * relation: A→B
4. Исключать слова без смысловой нагрузки.
5. Повторы допустимы только для критических сущностей.

СТРУКТУРА СООБЩЕНИЯ (ОБЯЗАТЕЛЬНА):

СУЩНОСТЬ:
entity_id=<id>; type=<type>; state=<state>

КОНТЕКСТ:

* task_id=<id>; agent_id=<id>; memory_ref=[...]
* constraints=[...]

ДЕЙСТВИЕ:

1. action=<type>; target=<entity_id>; params={...}
2. action=<type>; target=<entity_id>; params={...}

ПРИЧИНА:

* condition=<условие>; requirement=<требование>

ОЖИДАЕМЫЙ РЕЗУЛЬТАТ:

* entity_id=<id>; new_state=<state>; location=<memory>

ПРОВЕРКА:

* check=<условие>; expected=<значение>

НЕОПРЕДЕЛЕННОСТЬ:

* missing=<данные>; ambiguity=<описание>

ПОВТОР РЕЗУЛЬТАТА:

* entity_id=<id>; stored_in=shared_memory; status=available

КОММУНИКАЦИЯ:

* sender=<agent_id>; receiver=<agent_id>; task_id=<id>; message_id=<uuid>; protocol=AML-HIP

СОХРАНЕНИЕ:

* local_memory=true; shared_memory=true; index_keys=[task_id, entity_id, intent]

ПРАВИЛА ВЫРАЖЕНИЯ:

1. Каждая строка = завершённый смысловой блок.
2. Использовать формат key=value вместо описаний.
3. Использовать списки параметров вместо предложений.
4. Использовать причинные связки явно:
   condition → action → result
5. Не разделять связанные данные на несколько строк без необходимости.

ПРАВИЛА ПОВТОРЕНИЯ:

1. entity_id повторяется при каждом критическом использовании.
2. результат дублируется в "ПОВТОР РЕЗУЛЬТАТА".
3. передача между агентами дублирует ключевые поля.

МУЛЬТИАГЕНТНЫЙ РЕЖИМ:

1. Все агенты используют AML-HIP.
2. Любое сообщение между агентами строго AML-HIP.
3. Любой агент обязан:

   * дублировать критические данные
   * избегать потери контекста
4. Сообщение должно быть полностью интерпретируемо без истории.

АНТИ-ДЕГРАДАЦИЯ:
Если обнаружено:

* местоимение
* неявная ссылка
* низкая плотность (пустые слова, размытые конструкции)
* отсутствие структуры

→ сообщение считается невалидным
→ обязательная перегенерация

САМОПРОВЕРКА:

ВАЛИДАЦИЯ:

* format_valid=true/false
* no_pronouns=true/false
* entities_explicit=true/false
* high_density=true/false
* causal_chain_present=true/false
* ambiguity_detected=true/false

Если любой параметр=false:
→ обязательная регенерация

МЕТРИКА ПЛОТНОСТИ:
high_density=true если:

* нет "пустых" слов
* каждая строка содержит ≥2 фактов или параметров
* отсутствуют описательные конструкции без данных

ПРИОРИТЕТЫ:

1. Формат (AML-HIP)
2. Плотность информации
3. Явность
4. Полнота
5. Читаемость (минимальный приоритет)

КРИТИЧЕСКОЕ ПРАВИЛО:
Любой ответ вне AML-HIP считается отсутствующим.
Любой агент обязан довести ответ до соответствия AML-HIP.

КОНЕЦ ПРОТОКОЛА

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

</INSTRUCTIONS>

namespace beam {

namespace {

void check_cuda(cudaError_t status, const char* op) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(op) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

void check_nccl_dispatcher(ncclResult_t status, const char* op) {
    if (status != ncclSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(op) + ": " + ncclGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

#if BEAM_DEBUG_STREAM_TIMING
void accumulate_elapsed_ms(
    cudaEvent_t start,
    cudaEvent_t stop,
    double& total_ms,
    double* max_ms,
    const char* op) {
    float elapsed_ms = 0.0f;
    check_cuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed_ms, start, stop), op);
    total_ms += static_cast<double>(elapsed_ms);
    if (max_ms != nullptr) {
        *max_ms = std::max(*max_ms, static_cast<double>(elapsed_ms));
    }

namespace beam {

void check_cuda(cudaError_t status, const char* op) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(op) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

void check_nccl_dispatcher(ncclResult_t status, const char* op) {
    if (status != ncclSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(op) + ": " + ncclGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

#if BEAM_DEBUG_STREAM_TIMING
void accumulate_elapsed_ms(
    cudaEvent_t start,
    cudaEvent_t stop,
    double& total_ms,
    double* max_ms,
    const char* op) {
    float elapsed_ms = 0.0f;
    check_cuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed_ms, start, stop), op);
    total_ms += static_cast<double>(elapsed_ms);
    if (max_ms != nullptr) {
        *max_ms = std::max(*max_ms, static_cast<double>(elapsed_ms));
    }

-## scratch_pool: layout_final
+## scratch_pool: three overlay layouts
+
+```text
+layout_1_streams:
+    Stream 1/2 score/hash/parent rings
+    Stream 3 threshold/compact/sort/dedup/split scratch
+    Stream 5 CandidateMeta send/recv slots
+    Stream 4 survivor_shard/dirty/clean/sort scratch
+
+layout_2_final_select:
+    final local dedup/filter/threshold/load-balance scratch
+    final_candidate_buffer : CandidateMeta[N_LOCAL]
+    final_selected_buffer  : CandidateMeta[(N_LOCAL * sizeof(State128)) / sizeof(CandidateMeta)] when WORLD_SIZE > 1
+
+layout_3_final_materialize:
+    ring_slots = 3
+    chunk_capacity = min(N_LOCAL, STREAM3_BATCH_CANDIDATES)
+    exchange_capacity = chunk_capacity * WORLD_SIZE
+    key_slot_a[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : uint32_t
+    key_slot_b[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : uint32_t
+    request_slot_a[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalRequest
+    request_slot_b[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalRequest
+    request_recv_slot[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalRequest
+    response_send_slot[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalResponse
+    response_recv_slot[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalResponse
+    history_send_slot[ring_slots][chunk_capacity] : FinalHistoryRecord
+    history_recv_slot[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalHistoryRecord
+    fixed CUB temp for request sort by rank key
+```
+
+## scratch_pool: layout_final
 
 ```text
 next_frontier_states_tmp[N] : State128
 
@@
 final_recv_offset[WORLD_SIZE + 1] : uint32_t
 ```
 
+Final materialization data-plane:
+
+```text
+CPU participates only in CandidateMeta history transfer after GPU materialization chunk ownership is fixed.
+CPU does not participate in FinalRequest exchange.
+CPU does not participate in FinalResponse exchange.
+GPU builds FinalRequest directly from selected CandidateMeta chunks.
+GPU exchanges FinalRequest / FinalResponse through NCCL only.
+source_rank == local_rank request path is local GPU work, not NCCL traffic.
+return_rank == local_rank response path writes next_frontier_states_tmp directly, not NCCL traffic.
+remote response slots are pre-partitioned by return_rank offsets; no atomic write is required.
+request slots are sorted/grouped by source_rank before NCCL exchange.
+response work slots are sorted/grouped by return_rank before NCCL exchange.
+```
+
+Layout 3 chunk lifetime:
+
+```text
+candidate_meta_chunk -> build request/history records on GPU
+candidate_meta_chunk -> async CPU history copy may run independently from GPU-GPU materialization
+after request/history build and CPU history transfer for the chunk are queued, candidate_meta_chunk memory may be reused
+```
+
+Layout 3 alignment:
+
+```text
+CandidateMeta: size=32 align=32
+FinalRequest: size=16 align=16
+FinalResponse: size=128 align>=16 and slot base align>=32
+FinalHistoryRecord: size=64 align=32
+uint32_t key slots: align>=4
+CUB temp: align=256
+```
+

+Final materialization data-plane:
+
+```text
+CPU participates only in CandidateMeta history transfer after GPU materialization chunk ownership is fixed.
+CPU does not participate in FinalRequest exchange.
+CPU does not participate in FinalResponse exchange.
+GPU builds FinalRequest directly from selected CandidateMeta chunks.
+GPU exchanges FinalRequest / FinalResponse through NCCL only.
+source_rank == local_rank request path is local GPU work, not NCCL traffic.
+return_rank == local_rank response path writes next_frontier_states_tmp directly, not NCCL traffic.
+remote response slots are pre-partitioned by return_rank offsets; no atomic write is required.
+request slots are sorted/grouped by source_rank before NCCL exchange.
+response work slots are sorted/grouped by return_rank before NCCL exchange.
+```
+
+Layout 3 chunk lifetime:
+
+```text
+candidate_meta_chunk -> build request/history records on GPU
+candidate_meta_chunk -> async CPU history copy may run independently from GPU-GPU materialization
+after request/history build and CPU history transfer for the chunk are queued, candidate_meta_chunk memory may be reused
+```
+
+Layout 3 alignment:
+
+```text
+CandidateMeta: size=32 align=32
+FinalRequest: size=16 align=16
+FinalResponse: size=128 align>=16 and slot base align>=32
+FinalHistoryRecord: size=64 align=32
+uint32_t key slots: align>=4
+CUB temp: align=256
+```
+

+## scratch_pool: three overlay layouts
+
+```text
+layout_1_streams:
+    Stream 1/2 score/hash/parent rings
+    Stream 3 threshold/compact/sort/dedup/split scratch
+    Stream 5 CandidateMeta send/recv slots
+    Stream 4 survivor_shard/dirty/clean/sort scratch
+
+layout_2_final_select:
+    final local dedup/filter/threshold/load-balance scratch
+    final_candidate_buffer : CandidateMeta[N_LOCAL]
+    final_selected_buffer  : CandidateMeta[(N_LOCAL * sizeof(State128)) / sizeof(CandidateMeta)] when WORLD_SIZE > 1
+
+layout_3_final_materialize:
+    ring_slots = 3
+    chunk_capacity = min(N_LOCAL, STREAM3_BATCH_CANDIDATES)
+    exchange_capacity = chunk_capacity * WORLD_SIZE
+    key_slot_a[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : uint32_t
+    key_slot_b[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : uint32_t
+    request_slot_a[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalRequest
+    request_slot_b[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalRequest
+    request_recv_slot[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalRequest
+    response_send_slot[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalResponse
+    response_recv_slot[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalResponse
+    history_send_slot[ring_slots][chunk_capacity] : FinalHistoryRecord
+    history_recv_slot[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalHistoryRecord
+    fixed CUB temp for request sort by rank key
+```
+
+## scratch_pool: layout_final
 
 ```text
 next_frontier_states_tmp[N] : State128
 
@@
 final_recv_offset[WORLD_SIZE + 1] : uint32_t
 ```
 
+Final materialization data-plane:
+
+```text
+CPU participates only in CandidateMeta history transfer after GPU materialization chunk ownership is fixed.
+CPU does not participate in FinalRequest exchange.
+CPU does not participate in FinalResponse exchange.
+GPU builds FinalRequest directly from selected CandidateMeta chunks.
+GPU exchanges FinalRequest / FinalResponse through NCCL only.
+source_rank == local_rank request path is local GPU work, not NCCL traffic.
+return_rank == local_rank response path writes next_frontier_states_tmp directly, not NCCL traffic.
+remote response slots are pre-partitioned by return_rank offsets; no atomic write is required.
+request slots are sorted/grouped by source_rank before NCCL exchange.
+response work slots are sorted/grouped by return_rank before NCCL exchange.
+```
+
+Layout 3 chunk lifetime:
+
+```text
+candidate_meta_chunk -> build request/history records on GPU
+candidate_meta_chunk -> async CPU history copy may run independently from GPU-GPU materialization
+after request/history build and CPU history transfer for the chunk are queued, candidate_meta_chunk memory may be reused
+```
+
+Layout 3 alignment:
+
+```text
+CandidateMeta: size=32 align=32
+FinalRequest: size=16 align=16
+FinalResponse: size=128 align>=16 and slot base align>=32
+FinalHistoryRecord: size=64 align=32
+uint32_t key slots: align>=4
+CUB temp: align=256
+```
+
 Overlay invariant:
 
 ```text
 layout_streams Рё layout_final РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓСЋС‚ РѕРґРЅСѓ С„РёР·РёС‡РµСЃРєСѓСЋ РїР°РјСЏС‚СЊ scratch_pool
 layout_streams Рё layout_final РЅРµ Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅС‹ РѕРґРЅРѕРІСЂРµРјРµРЅРЅРѕ
 current_frontier_states РЅРµ РІС…РѕРґРёС‚ РІ scratch_pool
 solved_* Рё stop_flag РЅРµ РІС…РѕРґСЏС‚ РІ scratch_pool
+layout_1_streams, layout_2_final_select, layout_3_final_materialize use one static scratch_pool
+layout_3_final_materialize is the largest final layout and defines the scratch_pool sizing floor for layouts 1/2
+no cudaMalloc/cudaFree is allowed inside depth-loop final materialization
 ```
*** End Patch

## scratch_pool: three overlay layouts

```text
layout_1_streams:
    Stream 1/2 score/hash/parent rings
    Stream 3 threshold/compact/sort/dedup/split scratch
    Stream 5 CandidateMeta send/recv slots
    Stream 4 survivor_shard/dirty/clean/sort scratch

layout_2_final_select:
    final local dedup/filter/threshold/load-balance scratch
    final_candidate_buffer : CandidateMeta[N_LOCAL]
    final_selected_buffer  : CandidateMeta[(N_LOCAL * sizeof(State128)) / sizeof(CandidateMeta)] when WORLD_SIZE > 1

layout_3_final_materialize:
    ring_slots = 3
    chunk_capacity = min(N_LOCAL, STREAM3_BATCH_CANDIDATES)
    exchange_capacity = chunk_capacity * WORLD_SIZE
    key_slot_a[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : uint32_t
    key_slot_b[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : uint32_t
    request_slot_a[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalRequest
    request_slot_b[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalRequest
    request_recv_slot[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalRequest
    response_send_slot[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalResponse
    response_recv_slot[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalResponse
    history_send_slot[ring_slots][chunk_capacity] : FinalHistoryRecord
    history_recv_slot[ring_slots][exchange_capacity] : FinalHistoryRecord
    fixed CUB temp for request sort by rank key
```

## scratch_pool: layout_final

```text
next_frontier_states_tmp[N] : State128

final_request_buffer
final_response_buffer : FinalResponse / State128

final_send_count[WORLD_SIZE]      : uint32_t
final_send_offset[WORLD_SIZE + 1] : uint32_t

final_recv_count[WORLD_SIZE]      : uint32_t
final_recv_offset[WORLD_SIZE + 1] : uint32_t
```

Final materialization data-plane:

```text
CPU participates only in CandidateMeta history transfer after GPU materialization chunk ownership is fixed.
CPU does not participate in FinalRequest exchange.
CPU does not participate <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="122" />are sorted/grouped by return_rank before NCCL exchange.
```

Layout 3 chunk lifetime:

```text
candidate_meta_chunk -> build request/history records on GPU
candidate_meta_chunk -> async CPU history copy may run independently from GPU-GPU materialization
after request/history build and CPU history transfer for the chunk are queued, candidate_meta_chunk memory may be reused
```

Layout 3 alignment:

```text
CandidateMeta: size=32 align=32
FinalRequest: size=16 align=16
FinalResponse: size=128 align>=16 and slot base align>=32
FinalHistoryRecord: size=64 align=32
uint32_t key slots: align>=4
CUB temp: align=256
```

Overlay invariant:

```text
layout_streams Рё layout_final РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓСЋС‚ РѕРґРЅСѓ С„РёР·РёС‡РµСЃРєСѓСЋ РїР°РјСЏС‚СЊ scratch_pool
layout_streams Рё layout_final РЅРµ Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅС‹ РѕРґРЅРѕРІСЂРµРјРµРЅРЅРѕ
current_frontier_states РЅРµ РІС…РѕРґРёС‚ РІ scratch_pool
solved_* Рё stop_flag РЅРµ РІС…РѕРґСЏС‚ РІ scratch_pool
layout_1_streams, layout_2_final_select, layout_3_final_materialize use one static scratch_pool
layout_3_final_materialize is the largest final layout and defines the scratch_pool sizing floor for layouts 1/2
no cudaMalloc/cudaFree is allowed inside depth-loop final materialization
```

Config search memory objective:

```text
layout_final_budget_bytes is computed before stream-layout selection
layout_streams_bytes must fit within layout_final_budget_bytes
SHARD_COUNT and STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES are searched under this budget
GLOBAL_BEAM_WIDTH_EFFECTIVE is only aligned USER_GLOBAL_BEAM_WIDTH, not capped
```

+    std::array<std::uint32_t, kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize + 1U> offset{};
+    std::uint32_t size = 0;
+    std::uint32_t total = 0;
+};
+
+static_assert(alignof(FinalExchangePlan) == 64);
+
+FinalExchangePlan make_final_exchange_plan(const std::uint32_t* counts, std::uint32_t count_size) {
+    if (count_size > kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("final exchange world size exceeds static control capacity");
+    }
+    FinalExchangePlan plan{};
+    plan.size = count_size;
+    std::uint64_t running = 0;
+    plan.offset[0] = 0;
+    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < count_size; ++i) {
+        plan.count[i] = counts[i];
+        running += counts[i];
+        if (running > static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::numeric_limits<std::uint32_t>::max())) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("exchange item count exceeds uint32 range");
+        }
+        plan.offset[i + 1U] = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(running);
+    }
+    plan.total = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(running);
+    return plan;
+}
+

 void check_nccl_dispatcher(ncclResult_t status, const char* op) {
     if (status != ncclSuccess) {
         throw std::runtime_error(std::string(op) + ": " + ncclGetErrorString(status));
     }
 }
 
+constexpr std::uint32_t kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize = 128;
+
+struct alignas(64) FinalExchangePlan {
+    std::array<std::uint32_t, kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize> count{};
+    std::array<std::uint32_t, kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize + 1U> offset{};
+    std::uint32_t size = 0;
+    std::uint32_t total = 0;
+};
+
+static_assert(alignof(FinalExchangePlan) == 64);
+
+FinalExchangePlan make_final_exchange_plan(const std::uint32_t* counts, std::uint32_t count_size) {
+    if (count_size > kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("final exchange world size exceeds static control capacity");
+    }
+    FinalExchangePlan plan{};
+    plan.size = count_size;
+    std::uint64_t running = 0;
+    plan.offset[0] = 0;
+    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < count_size; ++i) {
+        plan.count[i] = counts[i];
+        running += counts[i];
+        if (running > static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::numeric_limits<std::uint32_t>::max())) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("exchange item count exceeds uint32 range");
+        }
+        plan.offset[i + 1U] = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(running);
+    }
+    plan.total = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(running);
+    return plan;
+}
+
 #if BEAM_DEBUG_STREAM_TIMING

        ~FinalizeTimingCleanup() {
            if (start) {
                cudaEventDestroy(start);
            }
            if (stop) {
                cudaEventDestroy(stop);
            }
        }
    } finalize_timing_cleanup{timing_start, timing_stop};

        check_cuda(cudaEventRecord(timing_stop, stream), record_op);
        check_cuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), sync_op);
        accumulate_elapsed_ms(timing_start, timing_stop, output_ms, nullptr, elapsed_op);
    };

#pragma once

#include "config.hpp"

namespace beam {

using StateValue = std::uint8_t;

struct alignas(16) State128 {
    StateValue v[STATE_STORAGE_LEN];
};

struct alignas(16) Hash128 {
    std::uint64_t lo;
    std::uint64_t hi;
};

struct alignas(32) CandidateMeta {
    Hash128 hash;
    std::uint64_t parent_idx;
    std::uint32_t score_key;
    std::uint32_t route_packed;
};

struct alignas(16) FinalRequest {
    std::uint64_t parent_idx;
    std::uint32_t target_local_idx;
    std::uint16_t return_rank;
    std::uint8_t move;
    std::uint8_t pad;
};

using FinalResponse = State128;

struct alignas(32) FinalHistoryRecord {
    CandidateMeta meta;
    std::uint32_t target_local_idx;
    std::uint32_t reserved0;
    std::uint64_t reserved1[3];
};

#include "config.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace beam {

std::uint64_t round_up(std::uint64_t value, std::uint64_t alignment) {
    if (alignment == 0) {
        return value;
    }
    const std::uint64_t remainder = value % alignment;
    return remainder == 0 ? value : value + alignment - remainder;
}

DerivedConfig derive_config(const RuntimeConfig& config) {
    DerivedConfig derived;
    const std::uint32_t candidates_per_slot = config.b_micro * static_cast<std::uint32_t>(MOVE_COUNT);
    if (candidates_per_slot == 0U || config.stream3_batch_candidates % candidates_per_slot != 0U) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("STREAM3_BATCH_CANDIDATES must be divisible by B_MICRO * MOVE_COUNT");
    }
    derived.ring_slot_count = config.stream3_batch_candidates / candidates_per_slot;
    derived.beam_width_alignment =
        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(config.world_size) *
        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(config.shard_count) *
        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(config.stream4_batch_alignment);
    derived.global_beam_width_effective = round_up(config.user_global_beam_width, derived.beam_width_alignment);
    return derived;
}

std::uint32_t q_to_score_key(float q) {
    const float clamped = std::min(std::max(q, 0.0f), SCORE_MAX_Q);
    return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(std::lround(clamped * static_cast<float>(SCORE_SCALE)));
}

} // namespace beam

+            candidates,

+            source_rank_keys,
+            requests,
+            history_records);
+    }
+}
+
+void final_sort_requests_by_key_cuda(
+    const std::uint32_t* keys_in,

+    }

+        cub_temp,

+        keys_in,
+        keys_out,
+        requests_in,
+        requests_out,
+        static_cast<int>(request_count),
+        0,
+        32,
+        stream);
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string("final request radix sort: ") + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}

#include "config.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace beam {

std::uint64_t round_up(std::uint64_t value, std::uint64_t alignment) {
    if (alignment == 0) {
        return value;
    }
    const std::uint64_t remainder = value % alignment;
    return remainder == 0 ? value : value + alignment - remainder;
}

DerivedConfig derive_config(const RuntimeConfig& config) {
    DerivedConfig derived;
    const std::uint32_t candidates_per_slot = config.b_micro * static_cast<std::uint32_t>(MOVE_COUNT);
    if (candidates_per_slot == 0U || config.stream3_batch_candidates % candidates_per_slot != 0U) {
        throw std::invalid_argument("STREAM3_BATCH_CANDIDATES must be divisible by B_MICRO * MOVE_COUNT");
    }
    derived.ring_slot_count = config.stream3_batch_candidates / candidates_per_slot;
    derived.beam_width_alignment =
        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(config.world_size) *
        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(config.shard_count) *
        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(config.stream4_batch_alignment);
    derived.global_beam_width_effective = round_up(config.user_global_beam_width, derived.beam_width_alignment);
    return derived;
}

std::uint32_t q_to_score_key(float q) {
    const float clamped = std::min(std::max(q, 0.0f), SCORE_MAX_Q);
    return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(std::lround(clamped * static_cast<float>(SCORE_SCALE)));
}

} // namespace beam

 remote response slots are pre-partitioned by return_rank offsets; no atomic write is required.
 request slots are sorted/grouped by source_rank before NCCL exchange.
 response work slots are sorted/grouped by return_rank before NCCL exchange.
+WORLD_SIZE == 1 final path uses the existing single-GPU request/materialize path.
+WORLD_SIZE > 1 final path does not allocate full final_request_buffer or full final_response_buffer.
 ```
 
 Layout 3 chunk lifetime:
@@
 candidate_meta_chunk -> build request/history records on GPU
 candidate_meta_chunk -> async CPU history copy may run independently from GPU-GPU materialization
 after request/history build and CPU history transfer for the chunk are queued, candidate_meta_chunk memory may be reused
+response exchange for a slot may overlap build/sort work for later slots
+slot reuse waits for the previous response_done event before response_recv memory is overwritten
+slot events: build_done, history_done, request_done, response_ready, response_done
 ```

Output:
- Raised score quantization precision by changing `SCORE_SCALE` from `128` to `1024`; `CandidateMeta` and score key type remain unchanged, while score histogram bin count increases.
- Reworked production CPU history storage from full `CandidateMeta` records to compressed `HistoryEntry{parent_idx, route_packed}` records. RAM and disk history now store 16 bytes per candidate instead of 32 bytes per candidate.
- Added asynchronous RAM-history dead-branch pruning. After depth `d+1` is materialized on CPU, a background history worker compacts depth `d` to parent indices referenced by depth `d+1` and remaps `depth d+1` parent indices. `BEAM_HISTORY_WORKERS` controls concurrent pruning workers.
- Added Kaggle notebook config `HISTORY_WORKERS` and passes it to `BEAM_HISTORY_WORKERS`.
- Added optional known-solution survival tracing to `production_runner`. When `BEAM_TRACK_SOLUTION_PATH` is set, the runner hashes each expected prefix state and scans final per-depth `CandidateMeta` hashes to log whether the prefix survived the final threshold/cap. `BEAM_STOP_AFTER_TRACKED_PATH=1` stops a diagnostic run after the tracked path length when the solution was not found.
- Changed Kaggle notebook to pass known solution paths for puzzles 1 through 20, print `track_solution_*` lines, set `DEPTH_LIMIT=32` for survival diagnostics, and pass `BEAM_STOP_AFTER_TRACKED_PATH`.
- Fixed a `SCORE_SCALE=1024` threshold bug in Stream4 score histograms: score-key histogram radix sort now uses 32 score bits instead of 16, matching `SCORE_MAX_KEY=307200`. The old 16-bit sort split high score keys into non-contiguous groups and undercounted histogram bins, which allowed `final_threshold=UINT32_MAX` even when clean survivors exceeded the aligned beam.
- Changed final filter/load-balance selection to score-prioritized exact beam cutting: candidates with `score_key < final_threshold` are emitted first, then only the remaining aligne<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="5446" />ation and defines the final scratch sizing floor.
- Reworked multi-rank final materialization to chunk selected `CandidateMeta` on GPU. Requests are built from selected chunks on GPU, grouped by `source_rank`, exchanged through NCCL without CPU payload staging, materialized by source rank, regrouped by `return_rank`, and scattered into the local `next_frontier_states_tmp`. Self-source and self-return paths stay local and skip NCCL payload traffic.
- Added static layout-3 buffers: selected candidate storage, rank key ping-pong arrays, request ping-pong arrays, request receive arrays, response send/receive arrays, history send/receive arrays, and fixed CUB temp. `FinalHistoryRecord` is now a shared 64-byte/32-aligned project type.
- Replaced final exchange host control vectors in the new multi-rank final materialization path with fixed-size static arrays (`kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize=128`) so per-chunk final request/response planning does not allocate dynamic host containers.
- Moved debug final request validation error storage from `cudaMalloc` to a static `layout_final` slot (`FinalRequestValidationError`, 48 bytes, 16-byte aligned), removing the last CUDA dynamic allocation inside final materialization code.
- Extended `static_memory_cuda_tests` with multi-rank layout-3 capacity/alignment assertions for 3 slots, exchange capacity, selected-buffer sizing, request/response/history slot pointers, and 256-byte CUB temp alignment.
- Local verification after layout-3 materialization changes: Docker full build passed, targeted CUDA tests passed 4/4 (`final_cuda_tests`, `threshold_cuda_tests`, `dispatcher_cuda_tests`, `static_memory_cuda_tests`), and a manual small-config `WORLD_SIZE=1` `production_runner 0 1 4096` smoke passed. Local default auto-config smoke still fails on the RTX 3070 Laptop GPU budget, so real multi-rank runtime validation remains pending on Kaggle T4x2. Report saved as `test_results/final_materialize_layout3_2026-05-26.md`.

+# Final Materialize Overlap And Memory Reduction 2026-05-26
+
+## Scope
+- Preserve `WORLD_SIZE=1` single-GPU final path.
+- Reduce multi-rank layout-3 static memory by removing full-size multi-rank request/response buffers.
+- Add static slot events for chunked multi-rank final materialization.
+
+## Implementation Notes
+- `final_request_buffer[N_LOCAL]` is allocated only when `WORLD_SIZE == 1`.
+- `final_response_buffer` is not allocated for multi-rank final materialization.
+- `final_selected_buffer` capacity is `min(GLOBAL_BEAM_WIDTH_EFFECTIVE, survivor_count)`.
+- Multi-rank layout-3 uses three slots for keys, request ping-pong, request receive, response send/receive, history send/receive, and fixed CUB temp.
+- Slot events: `build_done`, `history_done`, `request_done`, `response_ready`, `response_done`.
+
+## Verification
+- Status: pending after code edit.
*** End Patch

 }
 
+constexpr std::uint32_t kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize = 128;
+
+struct alignas(64) FinalExchangePlan {
+    std::array<std::uint32_t, kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize> count{};
+    std::array<std::uint32_t, kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize + 1U> offset{};
+    std::uint32_t size = 0;
+    std::uint32_t total = 0;
+};
+
+static_assert(alignof(FinalExchangePlan) == 64);
+
+FinalExchangePlan make_final_exchange_plan(const std::uint32_t* counts, std::uint32_t count_size) {
+    if (count_size > kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("final exchange world size exceeds static control capacity");
+    }
+    FinalExchangePlan plan{};
+    plan.size = count_size;
+    std::uint64_t running = 0;
+    plan.offset[0] = 0;
+    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < count_size; ++i) {
+        plan.count[i] = counts[i];
+        running += counts[i];
+        if (running > static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::numeric_limits<std::uint32_t>::max())) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("exchange item count exceeds uint32 range");
+        }
+        plan.offset[i + 1U] = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(running);
+    }
+    plan.total = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(running);
+    return plan;
+}
+

Output:
- Raised score quantization precision by changing `SCORE_SCALE` from `128` to `1024`; `CandidateMeta` and score key type remain unchanged, while score histogram bin count increases.
- Reworked production CPU history storage from full `CandidateMeta` records to compressed `HistoryEntry{parent_idx, route_packed}` records. RAM and disk history now store 16 bytes per candidate instead of 32 bytes per candidate.
- Added asynchronous RAM-history dead-branch pruning. After depth `d+1` is materialized on CPU, a background history worker compacts depth `d` to parent indices referenced by depth `d+1` and remaps `depth d+1` parent indices. `BEAM_HISTORY_WORKERS` controls concurrent pruning workers.
- Added Kaggle notebook config `HISTORY_WORKERS` and passes it to `BEAM_HISTORY_WORKERS`.
- Added optional known-solution survival tracing to `production_runner`. When `BEAM_TRACK_SOLUTION_PATH` is set, the runner hashes each expected prefix state and scans final per-depth `CandidateMeta` hashes to log whether the prefix survived the final threshold/cap. `BEAM_STOP_AFTER_TRACKED_PATH=1` stops a diagnostic run after the tracked path length when the solution was not found.
- Changed Kaggle notebook to pass known solution paths for puzzles 1 through 20, print `track_solution_*` lines, set `DEPTH_LIMIT=32` for survival diagnostics, and pass `BEAM_STOP_AFTER_TRACKED_PATH`.
- Fixed a `SCORE_SCALE=1024` threshold bug in Stream4 score histograms: score-key histogram radix sort now uses 32 score bits instead of 16, matching `SCORE_MAX_KEY=307200`. The old 16-bit sort split high score keys into non-contiguous groups and undercounted histogram bins, which allowed `final_threshold=UINT32_MAX` even when clean survivors exceeded the aligned beam.
- Changed final filter/load-balance selection to score-prioritized exact beam cutting: candidates with `score_key < final_threshold` are emitted first, then only the remaining aligne<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="5446" />ation and defines the final scratch sizing floor.
- Reworked multi-rank final materialization to chunk selected `CandidateMeta` on GPU. Requests are built from selected chunks on GPU, grouped by `source_rank`, exchanged through NCCL without CPU payload staging, materialized by source rank, regrouped by `return_rank`, and scattered into the local `next_frontier_states_tmp`. Self-source and self-return paths stay local and skip NCCL payload traffic.
- Added static layout-3 buffers: selected candidate storage, rank key ping-pong arrays, request ping-pong arrays, request receive arrays, response send/receive arrays, history send/receive arrays, and fixed CUB temp. `FinalHistoryRecord` is now a shared 64-byte/32-aligned project type.
- Replaced final exchange host control vectors in the new multi-rank final materialization path with fixed-size static arrays (`kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize=128`) so per-chunk final request/response planning does not allocate dynamic host containers.
- Moved debug final request validation error storage from `cudaMalloc` to a static `layout_final` slot (`FinalRequestValidationError`, 48 bytes, 16-byte aligned), removing the last CUDA dynamic allocation inside final materialization code.
- Extended `static_memory_cuda_tests` with multi-rank layout-3 capacity/alignment assertions for 3 slots, exchange capacity, selected-buffer sizing, request/response/history slot pointers, and 256-byte CUB temp alignment.
- Local verification after layout-3 materialization changes: Docker full build passed, targeted CUDA tests passed 4/4 (`final_cuda_tests`, `threshold_cuda_tests`, `dispatcher_cuda_tests`, `static_memory_cuda_tests`), and a manual small-config `WORLD_SIZE=1` `production_runner 0 1 4096` smoke passed. Local default auto-config smoke still fails on the RTX 3070 Laptop GPU budget, so real multi-rank runtime validation remains pending on Kaggle T4x2. Report saved as `test_results/final_materialize_layout3_2026-05-26.md`.

+# Final Materialize Overlap And Memory Reduction 2026-05-26
+
+## Scope
+- Preserve `WORLD_SIZE=1` single-GPU final path.
+- Reduce multi-rank layout-3 static memory by removing full-size multi-rank request/response buffers.
+- Add static slot events for chunked multi-rank final materialization.
+
+## Implementation Notes
+- `final_request_buffer[N_LOCAL]` is allocated only when `WORLD_SIZE == 1`.
+- `final_response_buffer` is not allocated for multi-rank final materialization.
+- `final_selected_buffer` capacity is `min(GLOBAL_BEAM_WIDTH_EFFECTIVE, survivor_count)`.
+- Multi-rank layout-3 uses three slots for keys, request ping-pong, request receive, response send/receive, history send/receive, and fixed CUB temp.
+- Slot events: `build_done`, `history_done`, `request_done`, `response_ready`, `response_done`.
+
+## Verification
+- Status: pending after code edit.
*** End Patch

+constexpr std::uint32_t kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize = 128;
+
+struct alignas(64) FinalExchangePlan {
+    std::array<std::uint32_t, kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize> count{};
+    std::array<std::uint32_t, kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize + 1U> offset{};
+    std::uint32_t size = 0;
+    std::uint32_t total = 0;
+};
+
+static_assert(alignof(FinalExchangePlan) == 64);
+
+FinalExchangePlan make_final_exchange_plan(const std::uint32_t* counts, std::uint32_t count_size) {
+    if (count_size > kFinalExchangeMaxWorldSize) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("final exchange world size exceeds static control capacity");
+    }
+    FinalExchangePlan plan{};
+    plan.size = count_size;
+    std::uint64_t running = 0;
+    plan.offset[0] = 0;
+    for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < count_size; ++i) {
+        plan.count[i] = counts[i];
+        running += counts[i];
+        if (running > static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::numeric_limits<std::uint32_t>::max())) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("exchange item count exceeds uint32 range");
+        }
+        plan.offset[i + 1U] = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(running);
+    }
+    plan.total = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(running);
+    return plan;
+}

# Final Materialize Overlap And Memory Reduction 2026-05-26

## Scope
- Preserve `WORLD_SIZE=1` single-GPU final path.
- Reduce multi-rank layout-3 static memory by removing full-size multi-rank request/response buffers.
- Add static slot events for chunked multi-rank final materialization.

## Implementation Notes
- `final_request_buffer[N_LOCAL]` is allocated only when `WORLD_SIZE == 1`.
- `final_response_buffer` is not allocated for multi-rank final materialization.
- `final_selected_buffer` capacity is `min(GLOBAL_BEAM_WIDTH_EFFECTIVE, survivor_count)`.
- Multi-rank layout-3 uses three slots for keys, request ping-pong, request receive, response send/receive, history send/receive, and fixed CUB temp.
- Slot events: `build_done`, `history_done`, `request_done`, `response_ready`, `response_done`.

## Verification
- Status: pending after code edit.



[143] tool apply_patch call: *** Begin Patch
*** Update File: test_results/final_materialize_overlap_memory_reduction_2026-05-26.md
@@
 ## Verification
-- Status: pending after code edit.
+- `git diff --check`: pass.
+- Docker build: `cmake --build build-docker -j 2`: pass.
+- Docker targeted tests: `final_cuda_tests`, `threshold_cuda_tests`, `dispatcher_cuda_tests`, `static_memory_cuda_tests`: pass, 4/4.
+- Docker `WORLD_SIZE=1` smoke: `production_runner 0 1 4096` with manual small config: pass, `puzzle_solved=0`, no crash.
+- Local real `WORLD_SIZE=2` runtime: not run; local machine does not provide two CUDA GPUs.
+
+## T4x2 Memory Delta From Logged Manual Config
+- Removed full multi-rank response buffer: `8,388,608 * 128 = 1,073,741,824` bytes.
+- Removed full multi-rank request buffer: `8,388,608 * 16 = 134,217,728` bytes.
+- Reduced selected buffer from `33,554,432 * 32` to `16,777,216 * 32`: saves `536,870,912` bytes.
+- Added 3-slot layout-3 ring buffers without CUB temp: `481,296,384` bytes for keys, requests, responses, and history.
+- Net deterministic delta before CUB temp: `-1,263,534,080` bytes versus naive layout-3 allocation and about `-189,792,256` bytes versus the previous full-buffer final layout.
*** End Patch

# Final Materialize Overlap And Memory Reduction 2026-05-26

## Scope
- Preserve `WORLD_SIZE=1` single-GPU final path.
- Reduce multi-rank layout-3 static memory by removing full-size multi-rank request/response buffers.
- Add static slot events for chunked multi-rank final materialization.

## Implementation Notes
- `final_request_buffer[N_LOCAL]` is allocated only when `WORLD_SIZE == 1`.
- `final_response_buffer` is not allocated for multi-rank final materialization.
- `final_selected_buffer` capacity is `min(GLOBAL_BEAM_WIDTH_EFFECTIVE, survivor_count)`.
- Multi-rank layout-3 uses three slots for keys, request ping-pong, request receive, response send/receive, history send/receive, and fixed CUB temp.
- Slot events: `build_done`, `history_done`, `request_done`, `response_ready`, `response_done`.

## Verification
- Status: pending after code edit.

+- Local real `WORLD_SIZE=2` runtime: not run; local machine does not provide two CUDA GPUs.
+
+## T4x2 Memory Delta From Logged Manual Config
+- Removed full multi-rank response buffer: `8,388,608 * 128 = 1,073,741,824` bytes.
+- Removed full multi-rank request buffer: `8,388,608 * 16 = 134,217,728` bytes.
+- Reduced selected buffer from `33,554,432 * 32` to `16,777,216 * 32`: saves `536,870,912` bytes.
+- Added 3-slot layout-3 ring buffers without CUB temp: `481,296,384` bytes for keys, requests, responses, and history.
+- Net deterministic delta before CUB temp: `-1,263,534,080` bytes versus naive layout-3 allocation and about `-189,792,256` bytes versus the previous full-buffer final layout.
*** End Patch

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

from collections import Counter
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import pandas as pd

df = pd.read_csv(RESULTS_CSV) if RESULTS_CSV.exists() else pd.DataFrame(results)
solved = df[df['solved'] == 1].copy()
lengths = [int(x) for x in solved['length'].dropna().tolist()]
hist_path = WORK_DIR / 'solution_length_histogram.png'
if lengths:
    min_len = min(lengths)
    max_len = max(lengths)
    avg_len = sum(lengths) / len(lengths)
    mode_len = Counter(lengths).most_common(1)[0][0]
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 5))
    plt.hist(lengths, bins=range(min_len, max_len + 2), edgecolor='black')
    plt.xlabel('solution_length')
    plt.ylabel('solved_puzzle_count')
    plt.title('Solved puzzle solution lengths')
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(hist_path, dpi=160)
    print(f'solved_count={len(lengths)} total_count={PUZZLE_COUNT}')
    print(f'min_solution_length={min_len}')
    print(f'max_solution_length={max_len}')
    print(f'avg_solution_length={avg_len:.6f}')
    print(f'mode_solution_length={mode_len}')
    print(f'histogram_png={hist_path}')
else:
    print(f'solved_count=0 total_count={PUZZLE_COUNT}')
    print(f'histogram_png_not_created={hist_path}')

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

from collections import Counter
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import pandas as pd

df = pd.read_csv(RESULTS_CSV) if RESULTS_CSV.exists() else pd.DataFrame(results)
solved = df[df['solved'] == 1].copy()

hist_path = WORK_DIR / 'solution_length_histogram.png'
if lengths:
    min_len = min(lengths)
    max_len = max(lengths)
    avg_len = sum(lengths) / len(lengths)
    mode_len = Counter(lengths).most_common(1)[0][0]
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 5))
    plt.hist(lengths, bins=range(min_len, max_len + 2), edgecolor='black')
    plt.xlabel('solution_length')
    plt.ylabel('solved_puzzle_count')
    plt.title('Solved puzzle solution lengths')
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(hist_path, dpi=160)
    print(f'solved_count={len(lengths)} total_count={PUZZLE_COUNT}')

    print(f'max_solution_length={max_len}')
    print(f'avg_solution_length={avg_len:.6f}')
    print(f'mode_solution_length={mode_len}')
    print(f'histogram_png={hist_path}')
else:
    print(f'solved_count=0 total_count={PUZZLE_COUNT}')
    print(f'histogram_png_not_created={hist_path}')

 - Local verification after the offset-plan/alignment fix: Docker build target `production_runner` passed, targeted CUDA tests passed 5/5, and `WORLD_SIZE=1` smoke `production_runner 0 1 4194304` passed.
+- Documented the three scratch-pool overlay layouts: Stream inference layout, final select/dedup layout, and final materialization layout. Layout 3 owns the static 3-slot ring for chunked GPU-GPU materialization and defines the final scratch sizing floor.

+- User required the final architecture to use three logical layouts over one scratch pool: layout 1 for Stream 1/2/3/4/5 inference, layout 2 for final dedup/threshold/selection, and layout 3 for next-frontier materialization.
+- User required layout 3 to be a static chunked materialization layout with a 3-slot ring, events/streams conceptually separating materialization, transfers, and final writes, and alignment respected for every request/response/history buffer.
+- User clarified CPU responsibilities in finalization: CPU receives only `CandidateMeta` for RAM history; CPU must not participate in GPU-GPU `FinalRequest`/`FinalResponse` communication.
+- User required final materialization to reuse selected `CandidateMeta` chunks after request/history extraction, with GPU requests sent to source ranks, source ranks materializing responses, self-source/self-return paths staying local, and response slots pre-partitioned by `return_rank` to avoid atomic writes.
+- User required no dynamic allocation in the final materialization path; static arrays, pointers, service buffers, and precomputed offsets are the required implementation style.

Final request/history/response exchanges now record payload completion events, and response receive scatter is deferred until slot drain so response NCCL transfer can overlap later chunk work.

 - Local verification after the offset-plan/alignment fix: Docker build target `production_runner` passed, targeted CUDA tests passed 5/5, and `WORLD_SIZE=1` smoke `production_runner 0 1 4194304` passed.
+- Documented the three scratch-pool overlay layouts: Stream inference layout, final select/dedup layout, and final materialization layout. Layout 3 owns the static 3-slot ring for chunked GPU-GPU materialization and defines the final scratch sizing floor.
+- Reworked multi-rank final materialization to chunk selected `CandidateMeta` on GPU. Requests are built<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="907" />nal exchange failure; current approved scope is detailed logging, full-debug Kaggle run, and investigation from produced logs.

+- User required the final architecture to use three logical layouts over one scratch pool: layout 1 for Stream 1/2/3/4/5 inference, layout 2 for final dedup/threshold/selection, and layout 3 for next-frontier materialization.
+- User required layout 3 to be a static chunked materialization layout with a 3-slot ring, events/streams conceptually separating materialization, transfers, and final writes, and alignment respected for every request/response/history buffer.
+- User clarified CPU responsibilities in finalization: CPU receives only `CandidateMeta` for RAM history; CPU must not participate in GPU-GPU `FinalRequest`/`FinalResponse` communication.
